#### `std::string generate(const std::string &, int, double, const std::string &) override`
- Source: `tests/ingestion/test_ingestion_ner_llm.cpp`:67
- Brief: Generate text from a prompt.
- Parameters:
  - `prompt` (const std::string &): The full prompt string (already assembled by caller).
  - `max_tokens` (int): Maximum number of tokens to generate.
  - `temperature` (double): Sampling temperature (lower = more deterministic).
  - `lora_adapter` (const std::string &): Optional LoRA adapter identifier / path. Empty string means no adapter.
- Return: Generated text. On unrecoverable error, implementations SHOULD return an empty string rather than throwing, so the ingestion pipeline can degrade gracefully. Exceptions are caught and wrapped by LegalLlmAdapter.

- Brief: n/a

#### `void BM_QJ01_EvaluateNullBackend(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/ingestion/bench_ingestion_quality_judge.cpp`:336
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures the overhead of evaluate() when the backend is unavailable. This is the "fail-open" guard path: the judge returns immediately with all scores = -1.0 and passed = true. No prompt building, no parsing. Expected: < 1 µs (branch + struct initialisation only).

#### `void BM_QJ02_EvaluateSingleDimension(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/ingestion/bench_ingestion_quality_judge.cpp`:363
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures the full evaluate() path with exactly one dimension active. Captures: prompt building (completeness), generate() stub call, parseScore(), parseRationale(), parseBulletList(), overall aggregation. Expected: < 10 µs (string ops + stub call overhead).

#### `void BM_QJ04_EvaluateSparseContext(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/ingestion/bench_ingestion_quality_judge.cpp`:440
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: When the document is too short or has too few entities the judge skips all LLM calls and returns immediately (fail-open). Identical performance target to QJ01.

#### `void BM_QJ05_EvaluateEntityScaling(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/ingestion/bench_ingestion_quality_judge.cpp`:474
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures how evaluate() overhead grows with the number of extracted entities. The entity summary (up to 30 labels) and relation summary are embedded in prompts — this test quantifies the string-formatting cost at scale. Argument: entity count per document.

#### `void BM_QJ06_EvaluateBulletListParsing(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/ingestion/bench_ingestion_quality_judge.cpp`:510
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures the overhead of parseBulletList() when the LLM response contains many missing-entity and hint entries. This is the worst-case parser path.

#### `void BM_QJ07_ObserverDispatch_Zero(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/ingestion/bench_ingestion_quality_judge.cpp`:556
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Baseline: evaluate() with no observers attached. Measures the pure judge overhead without any notification cost.

#### `void BM_QJ08_ObserverDispatch_N(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/ingestion/bench_ingestion_quality_judge.cpp`:592
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures the overhead of notifying N attached observers per evaluation. The observer implementation is a no-op to isolate the dispatch cost (lock acquisition + snapshot copy + virtual call loop). Argument: number of observers.

#### `void BM_QJ09_SetConfig(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/ingestion/bench_ingestion_quality_judge.cpp`:632
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures the cost of mutating a judge's configuration at runtime. In production this happens at most once per configuration reload cycle. Expected: < 500 ns (plain struct copy, no allocation).

#### `void BM_QJ10_ConstructDestruct(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/ingestion/bench_ingestion_quality_judge.cpp`:666
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Measures the overhead of constructing and immediately destroying an IngestionQualityJudge. In production the judge is created once per IngestionManager lifetime, so this is not on the critical path — it serves as a sanity check that there are no hidden allocations in the constructor. Expected: < 1 µs.

#### `void BM_QJ11_FeedbackLoopJudgeOnly(benchmark::State &state)`
- Source: `benchmarks/ingestion/bench_ingestion_quality_judge.cpp`:692
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `state` (benchmark::State &): n/a
- Details: Simulates the quality-judge side of the re-ingestion feedback loop (motivated by CRAG, Yan et al., ICLR 2024) without involving the WorkflowEngine — useful for isolating the judge's contribution to end-to-end latency as the number of re-evaluation passes grows. Argument: number of evaluate() passes in the loop.

### test_ingestion_highcardinality_stress.cpp

#### `TEST(IngestionHighCardinalityStress, BackpressureEdgeCaseStress)`
- Source: `tests/ingestion/test_ingestion_highcardinality_stress.cpp`:172
- Brief: BackpressureEdgeCaseStress.
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (IngestionHighCardinalityStress): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (BackpressureEdgeCaseStress): n/a
- Details: Fills the pipeline to capacity from 4 threads, then drains, and repeats 10 cycles. Asserts that each cycle's backpressure drop count is bounded and the pipeline recovers cleanly.

#### `TEST(IngestionHighCardinalityStress, ConcurrentSchemaValidationStress)`
- Source: `tests/ingestion/test_ingestion_highcardinality_stress.cpp`:140
- Brief: ConcurrentSchemaValidationStress.
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (IngestionHighCardinalityStress): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (ConcurrentSchemaValidationStress): n/a
- Details: Runs schema validation over 500 000 records from 8 concurrent threads. Asserts zero validation failures.

#### `TEST(IngestionHighCardinalityStress, HighCardinalityRecordIngest)`
- Source: `tests/ingestion/test_ingestion_highcardinality_stress.cpp`:103
- Brief: HighCardinalityRecordIngest.
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (IngestionHighCardinalityStress): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (HighCardinalityRecordIngest): n/a
- Details: Ingests 1 000 000 records from 8 concurrent producer threads into the in-process pipeline stub. Asserts that at least 1 000 000 records are either enqueued or counted as backpressure drops (no silent losses).

#### `BinaryMimeType detectBinaryMimeType(const std::string &raw)`
- Source: `src/ingestion/filesystem_ingester.cpp`:41
- Brief: Detect the binary MIME type of a buffer via magic-byte inspection.
- Parameters:
  - `raw` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Return: Detected type or BinaryMimeType::UNKNOWN
- Details: ------------------------------------------------------------------------ MIME type detection (free function – implementation) ------------------------------------------------------------------------ Reads the first 8 bytes of the provided raw buffer and identifies known binary formats: PDF: starts with PDF DOCX: starts with the ZIP magic PK 0x03 0x04 and contains the OOXML content-type marker in the first 512 bytes raw First bytes of the file (minimum 4 bytes required; fewer bytes always return BinaryMimeType::UNKNOWN) Detected type or BinaryMimeType::UNKNOWN raw Input parameter. Return value. Calls: size(), substr(), std::min(), size_t(), find().

- Parameters:
  - `code` (IngestionErrorCode): Input parameter.
- Return: true if the error indicates backpressure

Backpressure errors indicate that the component cannot accept more work and should temporarily reduce input rate. code Error code to classify true if the error indicates backpressure code Input parameter. True when the operation succeeds. Implements isBackpressureError without additional internal calls.

#### `bool isConverterSafe(const std::string &converter)`
- Source: `src/ingestion/filesystem_ingester.cpp`:77
- Brief: Check whether a converter program name/path is safe to use in a shell command.
- Parameters:
  - `converter` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Return: true if safe to pass to popen(); false if dangerous characters found
- Details: ------------------------------------------------------------------------ Converter path safety validation (free function – implementation) ------------------------------------------------------------------------ Rejects any value that contains shell metacharacters (\|, ;, &, $, <, >, ` , !, newlines, or NUL) that could enable command injection through the popen() call used to invoke external converters. An empty string returns true because an empty converter path means conversion is disabled (the file is silently skipped without spawning any process). converter Converter name or path as stored in BinaryConverter true if safe to pass to popen(); false if dangerous characters found converter Input parameter. True when the operation succeeds. Calls: empty().

#### `bool isPathTraversalSafe(const std::string &path)`
- Source: `src/ingestion/filesystem_ingester.cpp`:99
- Brief: Check whether a filesystem path is free of directory-traversal sequences.
- Parameters:
  - `path` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Return: true if no .. component is present; false if traversal detected
- Details: ------------------------------------------------------------------------ Path traversal safety validation (free function – implementation) ------------------------------------------------------------------------ Iterates the path components and returns false if any component equals ... This prevents a malicious or misconfigured SourceConfig::location from being used to escape an intended base directory (e.g. "../../etc/passwd"). An empty path returns true (no traversal sequence present; the caller is responsible for checking existence / accessibility separately). path Filesystem path to validate (absolute or relative) true if no .. component is present; false if traversal detected path Input parameter. True when the operation succeeds. Calls: empty(), fs::path().

#### `std::unordered_map< std::string, std::string > sanitizeOptions(const std::unordered_map< std::string, std::string > &options)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_manager.h`:256
- Brief: Return a copy of an options map with sensitive values redacted.
- Parameters:
  - `options` (const std::unordered_map< std::string, std::string > &): Source options map (as stored in SourceConfig::options)
- Return: Copy of the map with sensitive values masked
- Details: Keys whose names suggest they contain authentication credentials (api_key, token, oauth_client_secret, oauth_access_token, oauth_refresh_token, password, secret, client_secret, credentials, auth_token) have their values replaced with "***". All other key-value pairs are copied unchanged. Use this helper whenever an options map is included in log messages or error details to prevent accidental credential exposure in log output. options Source options map (as stored in SourceConfig::options) Copy of the map with sensitive values masked

#### `DeonticExtraction extract(const std::string &text) const`
- Source: `include/ingestion/deontic_extractor.h`:210
- Brief: Extract deontic information from a single text fragment.
- Parameters:
  - `text` (const std::string &): Raw German legal text (single provision or sentence)
- Return: Populated DeonticExtraction struct
- Details: Runs the configured extractor (regex by default) against the supplied text and returns a fully populated DeonticExtraction. #### `void setOAuthConfig(const OAuthConfig &config)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/api_connector.h`:177
- Brief: Configure OAuth 2.0 token refresh for this connector.
- Parameters:
  - `config` (const OAuthConfig &): Input parameter.
- Details: Set OAuth Config. When set, the connector will automatically attempt a token refresh (RFC 6749 §6) upon receiving HTTP 401. The refreshed access token is cached inside the connector and used for all subsequent requests in the same ingestion run. config.access_token is used immediately as the Bearer token; if it is empty, the static api_key (set via setApiKey() or options["api_key"]) is used instead until the first refresh. config OAuth configuration including token endpoint, client credentials, and the refresh token. config Input parameter. Implements setOAuthConfig without additional internal calls.

- Details: Gap 8 (AI_ML_IMPACT_ASSESSMENT. config Source configuration with: location: dataset name (e.g., "lexlms/ger_legal_data") options["split"]: dataset split (train/test/validation) options["streaming"]: "true" for streaming mode options["token"]: HuggingFace API token (optional) options["revision"]: dataset revision/branch (optional) true if initialization successful config Input parameter. True when the operation succeeds. md §7 — Severity: Medium/S1) — implemented 2026-04-21. The data classification gate is performed inside initialize() by calling ModelGovernancePolicy::checkExportPermission() with purpose="DATA_INGESTION" when an ingestion policy has been injected via setIngestionPolicy(). When no policy is set (nullptr), a WARN is logged and the gate is bypassed (degraded mode) to preserve backward compatibility with existing connectors. Tracked: src/ingestion/FUTURE_ENHANCEMENTS.md §"Data Classification Gate for External Connectors" Calls: hasIngestionPolicy(), find(), end(), checkIngestionPermission(), THEMIS_ERROR(), THEMIS_INFO(), THEMIS_WARN().

  - `config` (const OAuthConfig &): Input parameter.
- Details: Set OAuth Config. When set, the connector automatically attempts a token refresh (RFC 6749 §6) upon receiving HTTP 401 from the HuggingFace Hub API. The refreshed access token is cached inside the connector and used for all subsequent requests in the same ingestion run. config.access_token is used immediately as the Bearer token; if it is empty, the static API token (set via setApiToken() or options["token"]) is used until the first refresh. config OAuth configuration including token endpoint, client credentials, and the refresh token. config Input parameter. Implements setOAuthConfig without additional internal calls.

  - `validator` (DocumentValidatorFn): Callback to invoke per document; empty fn = disable
- Details: When set, the connector calls validator(content) for each document before counting it as processed. If the result is invalid and the source's SchemaConfig::reject_invalid flag is true, the document is counted as failed instead of processed. The default implementation is a no-op (validators are opt-in per connector). Concrete connectors that support field-level document validation should override this method. validator Callback to invoke per document; empty fn = disable

### themis::ingestion::ITensorDecompositionBackend

#### `TensorCoreRecord decompose(const std::vector< float > &embedding, const std::string &chunk_id, const std::string &source_file_id, double epsilon=0.01, std::size_t max_rank=0)=0`
- Source: `include/ingestion/inference_backend.h`:412
- Brief: Decompose a dense embedding vector into TT-format.
- Parameters:
  - `embedding` (const std::vector< float > &): Dense float32 embedding from a prior chunk_embed step.
  - `chunk_id` (const std::string &): Identifier linking result to the originating chunk.
  - `source_file_id` (const std::string &): SHA-256 digest of the originating file.
  - `epsilon` (double): Relative reconstruction error tolerance ε ∈ (0, 1]. Passed directly to TensorTrainDecomposer.
  - `max_rank` (std::size_t): Hard bond-dimension cap (0 = no cap).
- Return: Populated TensorCoreRecord. On error, returns a record with empty serialized_train so callers can degrade gracefully.
- Details: embedding Dense float32 embedding from a prior chunk_embed step. chunk_id Identifier linking result to the originating chunk. source_file_id SHA-256 digest of the originating file. epsilon Relative reconstruction error tolerance ε ∈ (0, 1]. Passed directly to TensorTrainDecomposer. max_rank Hard bond-dimension cap (0 = no cap). Populated TensorCoreRecord. On error, returns a record with empty serialized_train so callers can degrade gracefully.

#### `size_t retryAllQuarantine()`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_manager.h`:2013
- Brief: Retry all quarantined items that are not permanently failed.
- Parameters: none
- Return: Number of items that were successfully re-ingested
- Details: Retry All Quarantine. Iterates the quarantine list and calls retryQuarantineItem() for every entry whose permanently_failed flag is false. Number of items that were successfully re-ingested Return value. Calls: getQuarantineItems(), retryQuarantineItem().

#### `bool retryQuarantineItem(const std::string &item_path)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_manager.h`:2003
- Brief: Retry a single quarantined item with exponential back-off.
- Parameters:
  - `item_path` (const std::string &): Path to the item.
- Return: true if the item was successfully re-ingested and removed from quarantine; false if not found, permanently failed, or all retry attempts were exhausted
- Details: Retry Quarantine Item. When the entry has a raw_payload, the document is written directly without re-running the whole source. On each failed attempt the back-off delay is doubled (capped at RetryConfig::max_delay_ms). If retry_count reaches RetryConfig::max_quarantine_retries the entry is marked permanently_failed and excluded from future retries. If raw_payload is empty (legacy entry) the method falls back to re-running the originating source from the last checkpoint. item_path The quarantined item path/URL true if the item was successfully re-ingested and removed from quarantine; false if not found, permanently failed, or all retry attempts were exhausted item_path Path to the item. True when the operation succeeds. Calls: getQuarantineItems(), std::find_if(), begin(), end(), getRetryConfig(), empty(), std::max(), getDocumentWriteFn().

#### `IngestionBuilder & withApiSource(const std::string &source_id, const std::string &endpoint, std::unordered_map< std::string, std::string > options={}, int priority=5)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_manager.h`:1620
- Brief: Register a REST API source (supports cursor and offset/limit pagination).

- Return: *this for chaining
- Details: Registers a GenericApiConnector source. Pagination behaviour is controlled through the options map: Key Description Default api_key Bearer token for Authorization: Bearer <token> (none) page_size Items per page 100 pagination_mode "offset" (numeric) or "cursor" (opaque token) "offset" cursor_param Query-parameter name for the cursor / offset "offset" cursor_response_field JSON key in response containing the next cursor "next_cursor" text_field JSON key whose value is the document text "text" max_pages Maximum pages to fetch (0 = unlimited) 0 source_id Unique source identifier endpoint Base URL of the REST API endpoint options Optional key/value options (see table above) priority Source priority (default 5) *this for chaining

#### `IngestionBuilder & withCdcSource(const std::string &source_id, const std::string &connection_url, std::unordered_map< std::string, std::string > options={}, int priority=5)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_manager.h`:1764
- Brief: Register a CDC (Change-Data-Capture) source for live database streams.
- Parameters:
  - `source_id` (const std::string &): Unique source identifier
  - `connection_url` (const std::string &): Database connection URL (e.g. postgresql://host:5432/db)
  - `options` (std::unordered_map< std::string, std::string >): Optional key/value options (see table above)
  - `priority` (int): Source priority (default 5)
- Return: *this for chaining
- Details: Registers a CdcConnector source that consumes change events from a live database replication stream (PostgreSQL logical replication, MySQL binlog, or compatible CDC source) and ingests each event as a document. Supported options keys: Key Description Default slot_name Replication slot name (PostgreSQL) or equivalent themis_cdc table_filter Comma-separated table names to capture (empty = all) (all tables) operations Comma-separated ops to capture: INSERT,UPDATE,DELETE INSERT,UPDATE,DELETE text_columns Comma-separated columns to use as document text (full event JSON) batch_size Events per fetch batch 500 max_events Maximum events to consume (0 = unlimited) 0 poll_timeout_ms Poll timeout waiting for new events (milliseconds) 1000 from_lsn Start from this LSN / binlog position (empty = start) (from beginning) source_id Unique source identifier connection_url Database connection URL (e.g. postgresql://host:5432/db) options Optional key/value options (see table above) priority Source priority (default 5) *this for chaining

#### `IngestionReport ingestAll(const std::vector< SourceConfig > &sources, ProgressCallback progress_callback=nullptr)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_coordinator.h`:625
- Brief: Run distributed ingestion and return an aggregated report.
- Parameters:
  - `sources` (const std::vector< SourceConfig > &): Input parameter.
  - `progress_callback` (ProgressCallback): Input parameter.
- Return: Aggregated IngestionReport
- Details: Ingest All. Steps: Acquire (or renew) the leader lease — returns an empty report if this node cannot become leader within the configured TTL. Partition sources across worker nodes via partitionSources(). Dispatch each partition to the owning node concurrently. Aggregate partial reports and return the combined result. sources Sources to ingest progress_callback Optional callback forwarded to each worker node Aggregated IngestionReport sources Input parameter. progress_callback Input parameter. Return value.

#### `std::unordered_map< std::string, std::vector< SourceConfig > > partitionSources(const std::vector< SourceConfig > &sources) const`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_coordinator.h`:607
- Brief: Partition sources across worker nodes using consistent hashing.
- Parameters:
  - `sources` (const std::vector< SourceConfig > &): Sources to distribute
- Return: Map of node_id → sources assigned to that node. If the ring is empty, all sources are grouped under an empty key.
- Details: The source_id of each SourceConfig is hashed onto the ring. Sources that hash to the same node are grouped together. sources Sources to distribute Map of node_id → sources assigned to that node. If the ring is empty, all sources are grouped under an empty key.

- Details: Stop. Revokes the leader lease so that another node may take over immediately. Calls: exchange(), lk(), store(), notify_all(), joinable(), join(), revokeLease().

- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_manager.h`:1069
- Brief: Enable or disable incremental (resume-on-restart) mode.
- Parameters:
  - `enabled` (bool): true to enable incremental mode
- Details: When enabled and a checkpoint directory has been set, ingestion of a source skips documents up to the stored processed_count offset and resumes from there. A new checkpoint is written after each successful source run. enabled true to enable incremental mode

#### `void enableLineageTracking(bool enabled)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_manager.h`:1167
- Brief: Enable or disable end-to-end ingestion lineage tracking.
- Parameters:
  - `enabled` (bool): true to enable, false to disable
- Details: When enabled, each call to ingestSource() appends one or more IngestionLineageRecord objects to the internal IngestionLineageStore: Successful batch: one record with status = SUCCESS covering all documents written in that run. Each quarantined item: one record with status = QUARANTINED carrying the item's path as doc_id. Dry-run: one record with status = DRY_RUN; no writes occurred. Lineage tracking is disabled by default to avoid overhead when not needed. enabled true to enable, false to disable

#### `bool reconfigureSource(const std::string &source_id, const SourceConfig &new_config)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_manager.h`:851
- Brief: Update the configuration of an already-registered source at runtime.

  - `new_config` (const SourceConfig &): Input parameter.
- Return: true if the source was found and its configuration replaced
- Details: Reconfigure Source. Atomically replaces the stored SourceConfig for source_id with new_config. The change takes effect on the next call to ingestSource() or ingestAll() – no restart required. The new_config.source_id field is ignored; the source is always identified by the source_id parameter so callers cannot accidentally reassign an entry to a different key. source_id Identifier of the source to update new_config New configuration to apply true if the source was found and its configuration replaced false if no source with source_id is registered source_id Identifier of the source. new_config Input parameter. True when the operation succeeds. Implements reconfigureSource without additional internal calls.

  - `config` (const SchemaConfig &): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Schema Config. When a schema is set for source_id, every document produced by that source is validated via DocumentValidatorFn before being written to the target collection. Documents that fail validation are counted as failures; when SchemaConfig::reject_invalid is true (the default) they are also added to the quarantine queue. Call with a default-constructed SchemaConfig (or SchemaConfig{} with isEnabled() == false) to remove validation for a source. source_id Source whose documents the schema applies to config Schema rules to enforce source_id Identifier of the source. config Input parameter. Implements setSchemaConfig without additional internal calls.

#### `void setTextGenerationBackend(std::shared_ptr< ITextGenerationBackend > backend)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_manager.h`:1280
- Brief: Inject a text-generation backend into the ingestion pipeline.
- Parameters:
  - `backend` (std::shared_ptr< ITextGenerationBackend >): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Text Generation Backend. When set, runLegalExtraction() builds a LegalLlmAdapter backed by this backend and injects it into the SemanticValidator so deontic extraction uses LLM inference instead of regex. The ingestion module never sees a concrete LLM class — it only depends on ITextGenerationBackend (defined in ingestion/inference_backend.h). The LlmIngestionBridge (in llm/) is the only binding between the two modules and is provided by wiring code (main / server bootstrap). Passing nullptr (or not calling this method) resets to the default NullTextGenerationBackend, which falls back to regex extraction. Thread-safety: the backend pointer is stored once at startup; concurrent calls to runLegalExtraction() are safe as long as the backend itself is thread-safe (required by ITextGenerationBackend contract). Example (wiring code): #include"llm/llm_ingestion_bridge.h" autobridge=std::make_shared<LlmIngestionBridge>(); mgr.setTextGenerationBackend(bridge); backend Shared pointer to any ITextGenerationBackend. Pass nullptr to fall back to NullTextGenerationBackend. backend Input parameter. Calls: lock(), std::move().

#### `void setWorkflowEngine(std::shared_ptr<::themis::ingestion::WorkflowEngine > engine)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/ingestion_manager.h`:1311
- Brief: Inject the workflow orchestration engine.
- Parameters:
  - `engine` (std::shared_ptr<::themis::ingestion::WorkflowEngine >): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Workflow Engine. When a WorkflowEngine is set, calls to ingestFile() route through the YAML-driven pipeline (Stage 1–5 as described in ARCHITECTURE.md) instead of the legacy FileSystemIngester / runLegalExtraction() path. The legacy path remains fully functional when no WorkflowEngine is set (backward compatibility). Passing nullptr disables the workflow engine and reverts to the legacy path. Thread-safety: the engine pointer is stored once at startup; concurrent calls to ingestFile() are safe as long as the engine itself is thread-safe (guaranteed by WorkflowEngine). engine Shared pointer to a configured WorkflowEngine. Pass nullptr to revert to legacy mode. engine Input parameter. Calls: lock(), std::move().

#### `void setCheckpointStore(std::shared_ptr< CheckpointStore > store)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/kafka_connector.h`:147
- Brief: Inject a checkpoint store so that ThemisDB checkpoints and Kafka consumer-group offsets are committed in the correct order.
- Parameters:
  - `store` (std::shared_ptr< CheckpointStore >): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Checkpoint Store. When set, ingest() writes the ThemisDB checkpoint via the store before calling rd_kafka_consumer_close() (which commits Kafka offsets). This guarantees at-least-once delivery semantics: ThemisDB checkpoint written → database knows documents were processed. Kafka offsets committed → broker will not re-deliver messages. If the process crashes between steps 1 and 2, Kafka re-delivers the messages and ThemisDB deduplicates them via the checkpoint. The IngestionManager automatically injects the shared store whenever incremental mode is active. store Shared checkpoint store; pass nullptr to disable. store Input parameter. Calls: std::move().

#### `void setMessageBatchProvider(KafkaMessageFn fn)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/kafka_connector.h`:165
- Brief: Inject a Kafka message-batch provider.
- Parameters:
  - `fn` (KafkaMessageFn): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Message Batch Provider. When set, every Kafka poll that would normally be performed via librdkafka is replaced by a call to fn. Pass an empty KafkaMessageFn{} to restore the real librdkafka consumer. fn Input parameter. Calls: setMessageFetchForTesting(), std::move().

#### `DeonticExtractor::ExtractorFn buildExtractorFn() const`
- Source: `include/ingestion/llm_adapter.h`:176
- Brief: Build a DeonticExtractor::ExtractorFn for the configured model.
- Parameters: none
- Return: ExtractorFn (may be empty in Phase 1 / when no model is set)
- Throws:
  - std::runtime_error: if config_.model_path is set but the model file is not readable
- Details: Phase 1: returns an empty ExtractorFn{} when no LLM is available. Callers should check if (fn) before passing to setExtractorFn(). Phase 2: returns a callable that sends the text to the llama.cpp inference engine with a structured German legal extraction prompt and parses the JSON response into a DeonticExtraction. Throws when a model path is explicitly configured but the file cannot be opened. This keeps misconfigured deployments fail-closed instead of silently degrading to regex extraction. The returned function captures the model configuration by value so it remains valid independently of the LegalLlmAdapter instance lifetime. ExtractorFn (may be empty in Phase 1 / when no model is set) std::runtime_error if config_.model_path is set but the model file is not readable

- Details: Returns a structured prompt suitable for instruction-tuned models. The prompt is in German to leverage legal-domain knowledge.

- Source: `include/ingestion/inference_backend.h`:467
- Brief: Decompose a dense embedding vector into TT-format.
- Parameters:
  - `embedding` (const std::vector< float > &): Dense float32 embedding from a prior chunk_embed step.
  - `chunk_id` (const std::string &): Identifier linking result to the originating chunk.
  - `source_file_id` (const std::string &): SHA-256 digest of the originating file.
  - `epsilon` (double): Relative reconstruction error tolerance ε ∈ (0, 1]. Passed directly to TensorTrainDecomposer.
  - `max_rank` (std::size_t): Hard bond-dimension cap (0 = no cap).
- Return: Populated TensorCoreRecord. On error, returns a record with empty serialized_train so callers can degrade gracefully.
- Details: embedding Dense float32 embedding from a prior chunk_embed step. chunk_id Identifier linking result to the originating chunk. source_file_id SHA-256 digest of the originating file. epsilon Relative reconstruction error tolerance ε ∈ (0, 1]. Passed directly to TensorTrainDecomposer. max_rank Hard bond-dimension cap (0 = no cap). Populated TensorCoreRecord. On error, returns a record with empty serialized_train so callers can degrade gracefully.

Pagination continues until all objects under the prefix have been processed or max_keys is reached. Implements ingest without additional internal calls.

#### `IngestionStats ingest(const std::string &target_collection, ProgressCallback progress_callback) override`
- Source: `include/ingestion/s3_connector.h`:140
- Brief: List objects and ingest each one as a document.
- Parameters:
  - `target_collection` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `progress_callback` (ProgressCallback): Input parameter.
- Return: Return value.
- Details: Ingest. Pagination uses ListObjectsV2 continuation tokens. Each listed object is downloaded (up to max_concurrent_downloads in parallel) and its content is extracted via FileSystemIngester's format readers (for .jsonl, .csv, .parquet, .json, .txt, etc.) or treated as raw text for unknown extensions. When THEMIS_ENABLE_S3 is not defined and no mock is injected, returns immediately with a CONNECTOR_NOT_SUPPORTED error. target_collection Input parameter. progress_callback Input parameter. Return value. Implements ingest without additional internal calls.

#### `void setExtractor(DeonticExtractor extractor)`
- Source: `include/ingestion/semantic_validator.h`:286
- Brief: Replace the internal DeonticExtractor with a pre-configured one.
- Parameters:
  - `extractor` (DeonticExtractor): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Extractor. SoC / DIP noteThe default extractor uses built-in regex rules. To activate an LLM-backed extractor, build one via LegalLlmAdapter::buildExtractor() and inject it here. The SemanticValidator remains entirely free of any llm/ or LegalLlmAdapter knowledge — the caller provides the pre-built extractor. Example: //Inwiringcode(notiningestion/): LegalLlmAdapteradapter(bridge); DeonticExtractorllm_extractor=adapter.buildExtractor(0.75); SemanticValidatorvalidator; validator.setExtractor(std::move(llm_extractor)); extractor Pre-configured DeonticExtractor (moved in). extractor Input parameter. Calls: std::move().

#### `std::shared_ptr< IIngestionStep > createChunkEmbedStep(std::shared_ptr< IEmbeddingBackend > backend=nullptr)`
- Source: `src/ingestion/steps/chunk_embed_step.cpp`:123
- Brief: Create a builtin.chunk_embed step.
- Parameters:
  - `backend` (std::shared_ptr< IEmbeddingBackend >): Input parameter.
- Return: Return value.
- Details: Create Chunk Embed Step. For every TextChunk in ctx.chunks, computes a dense embedding via backend and appends a VectorRecord to ctx.embeddings. If backend is nullptr, a NullEmbeddingBackend (768-d, zero vectors) is used automatically; isAvailable() will be false so no real inference occurs but the step succeeds without error — useful for tests. Config keys (all optional): skip_when_unavailable bool default true — if true and backend is unavailable, the step is a no-op instead of an error. dims number Hint for NullEmbeddingBackend dimensionality (default 768). backend Injectable IEmbeddingBackend; nullptr → NullEmbeddingBackend. backend Input parameter. Return value.

#### `std::shared_ptr< IIngestionStep > createChunkTtDecomposeStep(std::shared_ptr< ITensorDecompositionBackend > backend=nullptr)`
- Source: `src/ingestion/steps/chunk_tt_decompose_step.cpp`:173
- Brief: Create a builtin.chunk_tt_decompose step.
- Parameters:
  - `backend` (std::shared_ptr< ITensorDecompositionBackend >): Input parameter.
- Return: Return value.
- Details: Create Chunk Tt Decompose Step. For every VectorRecord in ctx.embeddings that passes the κ-gate, computes a Tensor-Train decomposition via backend and appends a TensorCoreRecord to ctx.tensor_cores. Ordering constraint: this step MUST run after builtin.chunk_embed so that ctx.embeddings is already populated. If backend is nullptr, a NullTensorDecompositionBackend (isAvailable() == false) is used; the step becomes a no-op unless skip_when_unavailable is set to false. Config keys (all optional): skip_when_unavailable bool default true epsilon number TT-SVD error tolerance ε (default 0.01) max_rank number Bond-dimension cap (0 = no cap, default 0) min_kappa number Minimum compression ratio for κ-gate (default 1.3; set 0.0 to decompose all) backend Injectable ITensorDecompositionBackend; nullptr → NullTensorDecompositionBackend. backend Input parameter. Return value.

- Brief: Create a builtin.decompress step.
- Parameters: none
- Return: Return value.
- Details: Create Decompress Step. Unpacks ZIP / tar / gzip archives without a FormatExtractor dependency. Uses fork/execvp to invoke unzip or tar from the system PATH. Populates ExtractionContext::extracted_file_paths with the paths of the files written to a temporary directory. Config keys (all optional): output_dir string Base directory for extraction (default: /tmp/themis_decompress_XXXXXX) max_depth number Maximum recursive unpack depth (default: 1, i.e. no recursion) Return value. Implements createDecompressStep without additional internal calls.

Config keys (all optional): known_laws array of strings Pre-populated law IDs (e.g. ["BImSchG","StGB"]) Return value. Implements createLegalReferenceExtractorStep without additional internal calls.

- Return: Return value.

If sink is nullptr, an InMemoryTensorCoreBridge is used; records are never persisted across restarts (suitable only for tests). Config keys (all optional): tenant_id string Overrides tenant resolution when non-empty. Falls back to ctx.extra["tenant_id"], then to "default". skip_empty bool Skip records with empty serialized_train (default true). fail_on_write_error bool Propagate write errors as step failures (default false — records warned but skipped). require_persistent_sink bool Abort step when the sink is InMemoryTensorCoreBridge (default false). sink Injectable ITensorCoreBridge; nullptr → InMemoryTensorCoreBridge. sink Input parameter. Return value.
